Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the navier-stokes framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 743. The resulting operator achieves bound O(310/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the navier-stokes decomposition. By minimality, the element a^743 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 310-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
